}
mwp_d21_resource_current_trigger = yes
var:mwp_d21j_context_owner = this
var:mwp_d21j_context_day = days_since_birth
var:mwp_d21j_context_mode = mwp_auto_budget_mode_value
var:mwp_d21j_context_counter = mwp_native_cost_purchase_count_value
var:mwp_d21j_context_levies = var:mwp_d21r_max_levies

var:mwp_d21j_context_max = var:mwp_d21j_live_max

var:mwp_d21j_context_wallet_1 = gold
var:mwp_d21j_context_wallet_2 = prestige
var:mwp_d21j_context_wallet_3 = piety
var:mwp_d21j_context_wallet_4 = treasury
var:mwp_d21j_context_wallet_5 = barter_goods
var:mwp_d21j_context_balance_1 = var:mwp_d21r_gold_balance
var:mwp_d21j_context_balance_2 = var:mwp_d21r_prestige_balance

var:mwp_d21j_context_raised = mwp_d21_reject_raised_value
var:mwp_d21j_context_domicile = var:mwp_d21j_live_domicile

} }

}
trigger_else = { always = no }
